# Bug Report: BUG-002886

```yaml
id: e22a76dc-869e-4a33-ba3b-f25a4759307e
display_id: BUG-002886
status: new
module: startup-studio / events
category: bug
reported_at: 2026-03-08T05:39:23.837395+00:00
```

## Reporter
- **Name:** SYLANDERAN S
- **Email:** sylanderan.s@jkkn.ac.in
- **Role:** student

## Page URL
`https://www.jkkn.ai/startup-studio/events/572a5836-58a6-4f98-a3f4-92b862dd8080/my-team`

**Institution:** JKKN College of Pharmacy

## Description
Bug Title:
Team Leader Unable to Delete Created Team in Event Module – JKK N AI Web Application

Bug Type: Functional Bug / Permission Issue

Environment:

- Platform: JKK N AI Web Application
- Device: Android / Web Browser
- Browser: (Chrome / Edge – specify if required)
- Date Observed: 08 March 2026

Module: Event Participation / Team Management

Description:
While registering for an event in the JKK N AI web application, multiple team members accidentally created separate teams instead of joining a single team. In such cases, the team leader who created a team does not have the option or permission to delete that team.

This creates duplicate teams and prevents proper team management during event participation.

Steps to Reproduce:

1. Login to the JKK N AI web application.
2. Navigate to the Event section.
3. Create a new team as a Team Leader.
4. Observe the team management options.
5. Attempt to delete the team created by the team leader.

Actual Result:
There is no option available for the team leader to delete the team they created.

Expected Result:
The team leader who created the team should have permission to delete the team before the event registration is finalized.

Impact:

- Duplicate teams are created accidentally.
- Team members cannot reorganize their teams.
- Event management becomes confusing for participants.

Suggested Fix:
Provide a “Delete Team” option in the Team Management section, accessible only to the team leader who created the team or the system administrator.

Priority: Medium

Severity: Functional usability issue affecting event participation workflow.

## Console Logs
```
[LOG] All permissions are false - showing only Dashboard
[DEBUG] #1 1ms Starting document clone with size 360x1825 scrolled to 0,0
[DEBUG] #1 3114ms Document cloned, using foreign object rendering
[DEBUG] #1 3118ms EXPERIMENTAL ForeignObject renderer initialized (360x1825 at 0,0) with scale 1.5
[DEBUG] #1 3810ms Finished rendering
```
